22:T938,window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
window.gtag = window.gtag || gtag;
// analytics_storage default. Cookiebot loads afterInteractive, so a blanket
// 'denied' here left every page load with a dead window (until uc.js booted)
// in which implied-consent visitors who bounced or clicked through to
// app.runwayml.com produced zero analytics and no attribution cookie.
// Resolve synchronously instead — mirrors hasSynchronousCategoryConsent() in
// src/lib/cookiebotConsent.ts (vanilla JS duplicate: this runs before any
// bundle loads):
//   1. Cookiebot's own first-party CookieConsent cookie — a returning
//      visitor's stored decision (accept OR decline) always wins.
//   2. rw_consent_region, stamped by the proxy from x-vercel-ip-country:
//      'implied' (US/ROW) grants; 'explicit' (EU/EEA/UK/CH) denies.
//   3. No cookie at all (e.g. local dev): denied, wait for Cookiebot.
// Ad keys stay denied for everyone until Cookiebot updates them, which also
// preserves the old GPC posture (GPC only ever gated the ad stack).
function rwReadCookie(name) {
  var m = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + name + '=([^;]*)'));
  return m ? m[1] : null;
}
var rwStoredStats = null;
var rwCbStored = rwReadCookie('CookieConsent');
if (rwCbStored) {
  try {
    var rwCbMatch = decodeURIComponent(rwCbStored).match(/statistics:(true|false)/);
    if (rwCbMatch) rwStoredStats = rwCbMatch[1] === 'true';
  } catch (e) {}
}
var rwRegion = rwReadCookie('rw_consent_region');
var rwAnalyticsDefault =
  (rwStoredStats === null ? rwRegion === 'implied' : rwStoredStats)
    ? 'granted'
    : 'denied';
gtag('consent', 'default', {
  ad_storage: 'denied',
  ad_user_data: 'denied',
  ad_personalization: 'denied',
  analytics_storage: rwAnalyticsDefault,
  functionality_storage: 'granted',
  security_storage: 'granted',
  // Cookiebot loads afterInteractive (to avoid the banner's hydration
  // mismatch) and issues its consent 'update' fairly late. 500ms expired
  // before the grant arrived, so Consent Mode tags evaluated at 'denied' on
  // the page_view trigger and never re-fired — 0 tags fired all session.
  // 2000ms covers Cookiebot's grant latency so the tags wait for it.
  wait_for_update: 2000,
});
gtag('set', 'ads_data_redaction', true);

30:Ta08,Cinematic magical realism 5-shot sequence. A MERIDIAN coffee cup on a sunlit café table, real miniature clouds floating above it from the steam. The magical realism: the clouds are a living transport system — they carry the camera from the café to the high-altitude coffee farms of Colombia, floating over misty Andean slopes covered in coffee plants, before scooping up the essence of the place and drifting back to deposit it into the cup. A journey there and back, told through clouds.

SHOT 1 [0–2s]: Wide café — the MERIDIAN cup on the table, two small perfect clouds hovering above it, rotating slowly. One cloud begins to grow, darkening slightly, purposeful. It drifts toward the café window and presses through the glass. Sound: café ambient, the cloud humming with intent, a shift in pressure.

SHOT 2 [2–5s]: The cloud is now outside and rising rapidly — above the city, above the ocean, accelerating south. The camera rides with it. Below, green coastlines give way to the Andes rising from the earth, mist-covered peaks, and then — the Colombian coffee region: impossibly steep green hillsides terraced with rows of coffee plants in deep emerald and red, small farms dotted across the slopes, mist hanging in the valleys, golden morning light breaking over the ridgeline. Sound: wind and altitude, the world below, the coffee farms breathing.

SHOT 3 [5–8s]: The cloud descends slowly over the coffee farm — drifting between rows of coffee plants heavy with bright red cherries, the plants reaching up into the mist, a farmer moving between the rows in the early light. The cloud absorbs the red cherry colour, the soil smell, the mountain air, the morning — visibly deepening in colour, warming from white to the faintest amber-brown. Sound: farm birds, coffee cherries in the breeze, the mist, soil and altitude and rain all at once.

SHOT 4 [8–11s]: The amber-tinged cloud rises again, now full and heavy with the essence of the farm, and begins its journey back — rushing north over ocean and city, shrinking as it travels, carrying everything it collected.

SHOT 5 [11–15s]: Back in the café — the cloud descends into the MERIDIAN cup, dissolving into the coffee, releasing everything it carried: the altitude, the cherry, the mist, the soil, the morning. The coffee glows faintly richer. A pair of hands lifts the cup. One sip. Their eyes close.
